2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
 隨著法國距離下屆總統大選僅剩六個月，極左翼與政府間的政治對立升溫，然而
法國政府急需推動預算案以控制財政赤字與龐大債務。在內政危機加劇與工會
預告 11月大罷工的背景下，市場對法國債務可持續性的擔憂升溫，將帶動法德
公債利差維持高檔，進而推升歐元區主權風險溢酬並拖累歐元表現。
 上週路透最新調查顯示，在 100 位受訪經濟學家中，高達 94%預期聯準會將於
10 月會議維持利率在 3.75%-4.00% 不變，惟全員一致預估 12 月將重啟升息。
儘管美國受 AI 投資熱潮帶動、經濟成長強勁，且美伊戰事推升油價突破百元
大關、加徵關稅使 PCE 通膨至少在 2028 年前難以回到 2% 目標，官員仍傾
向審慎行事。不過逾四成專家警告，聯準會恐落後通膨形勢，後續面臨至少再升
息兩次甚至擴大升息幅度的上行風險，預期美元大幅回檔的空間小，逢低承接買
盤依然強勁。
 路透最新調查顯示，受日圓疲軟與中東戰事推升能源及進口成本、加劇通膨壓力

影響，近七成受訪經濟學家預計日本央行將於 12月再度升息至 1.50%，延續 6
月與 9 月以來的較快緊縮步伐；同時，逾半數專家預估終端利率將上看 2.00%
或更高。不過，高達近四分之三受訪者警告，若高市早苗內閣提名的鴿派委員再
次投下反對票，恐壓抑日圓表現。在歐美央行年底前亦可能升息的背景下，日本
央行維持較快的緊縮節奏，有助於防止美日、歐日利差進一步擴大，避免日圓在
年底遭遇新一輪劇烈拋售，為日圓提供實質的下檔保護。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Oct/27/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週台幣匯價高點為 32.360，低點在 32.704，以 32.508作收，較前一周收盤價
貶值 1 角 7 分。 
上週台幣走貶，周初，外資延續前周大幅匯出的走勢，台幣跳空開低，跌破今
年 7 月的低點 32.528；隨著台股表現不佳，外資賣超台股並匯出，台幣續創低位，
最低來到 32.704周內低點；而後台股低檔反彈，外資由匯出轉為雙向進出，加以出
口商積極進場拋售美金，台幣由貶轉升至 32.500下方；臨近周末，市場擔憂颱風來
襲周五可能休市，增添連續假期變數，外資再度大幅賣超台股並匯出，令台幣又跌
回 32.600上方，終場接在央行調節之下收在 32.508。 
＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣走勢偏弱。近期全球科技股因 AI 類股估值偏高及融資風險疑慮
而持續修正，市場風險偏好降溫，股市面臨修正，外資連續賣超台股並匯出，台幣
走勢持續面臨壓力。另一方面，美伊戰事和談進展的消息反覆，令國際油價節節攀
升，突破每桶 110美元上方，引發通膨升溫的擔憂，支撐利率維持在高檔的預期，
美元指數也在 101.5 上方高位震盪，將不利台幣的表現。盤勢上來看，外資隨著股
市回檔修正表現偏向賣超台股並匯出，台幣偏弱，不過本周適逢月底，預計匯價逢
高仍持續有出口商拋售美金的需求，將限制台幣走貶的幅度。綜合以上，預估本週
台幣走勢偏弱。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.300-32.800

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Oct/27/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣走升。周初中國公布9月工業生產物價指數 5.5%，表現優於預期的
5.4%(前值 5.2%)，且第三季 GDP表現 4.5%，也優於預期的 4.4%(前值 4.3%)，基本
面穩健支撐人民幣升破 6.6800 整數關口；不過周間美元指數持續於 101.5 上方高位
震盪，加以美伊戰事未見緩解，國際油價持續飆升，突破每桶 110 美元上方，人民
幣於 6.6800下方高檔震盪；臨近周末，人行中間價格連續走升，升破6.7200整數關
口，週五最高來到 6.7180，顯示中國人行允許人民幣溫和升值的走勢，終場境外人
民幣升破 6.6700整數關口 ， 收在6.6690， 週線升值160點 ， 境內人民幣則收在6.6704，
週線升值 166 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣偏升。就中國國內市場來看，人行中間價持續偏升，將持續帶
動人民幣走升，加以出口商實質拋匯需求將支撐人民幣表現；就國際市場來看，最
新利率期貨顯示 ， 本周聯準會利率決議有9成的機率維持利率在 3.75%~4.00%不變 ，
不過年底前仍有一碼的升息空間，加以地緣政治風險未見緩解，能源價格高檔震盪
推升通膨與利率預期，美元指數維持在 101.5 上方高檔震盪，將限制人民幣升值的
速度跟幅度；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH日線圖中，各天期均線向下發散，匯價
維持在五日均線之下交投，均線呈現空頭排列，顯示空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預
估本週人民幣偏升。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6300-6.6900

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Oct/27/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 USD/JPY 震盪下跌。週初，USD/JPY 大致在 158 的整數關卡上下波動；
週間，AI相關概念股遭拋售，加之中東局勢緊張推升避險需求，10年期美債殖利率
跌破 5%，避險資金亦流入日圓，帶動 USD/JPY 下行最低來至 156.67；臨近週末，
市場避險需求持續加之日本 9 月通膨數據高於日本央行 2%目標，USD/JPY 一度下
跌至 156.06，後逢低買盤進場跌幅有所收斂，終場收在 156.86，週線下跌 113點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY區間震盪，主要原因如下： 一、美國10 月 S&P製造業 PMI
為 56.0高於 9 月的 55.9且為 53個月高點，服務業則為 57.9低於 9月的 58.8，綜合
PMI為 57.6亦低於 9 月的 58.4，顯示美國商業活動雖略有放緩但仍處於擴張，經濟
基本面依然穩健將持續支撐美元；此外，中東局勢惡化使能源供應風險上升，布蘭
特原油價格突破 116美元，為六個月以來高點，也將有利USD/JPY上行。二、日本
9 月全國整體消費者物價指數（CPI）年增 2.1%，且 9 月核心 CPI年增 2.1%，為九
個月以來首次超過日本央行 2%的目標，強化市場對日本央行短期升息的預期，預計
為日圓帶來支撐。三、就技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY匯價處於各天期均線之
下且接連跌破 158 及 157 關卡，後在 156 整數關卡暫時獲得支撐，顯示逢低仍有買
盤進場，將限縮下行幅度。綜合以上，預估USD/JPY本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較
高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：155.00-158.50 
前週交易區間 
156.06 158.59

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
         Oct/27/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 EUR/USD震盪下跌。週初，EUR/USD在 1.1200整數關卡窄幅波動；週間
受到油價及美債殖利率回落，外加法國社會局勢略見緩解影響，帶動匯價反彈至
1.1260遇到賣壓後回落，後續大致在1.1190至 1.1250間波動；臨近週末，歐元區10
月份 PMI表現低迷，且市場對於法國政局和財政赤字擔憂持續，拖累 EUR/USD轉
若，匯價一度下行來至 1.1183，終場收在 1.1202，週線下跌 37點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD震盪偏空，主要原因如下：一、上週公佈歐元區10月 PMI
顯示德、法表現分化，法國製造業及服務業均表現疲弱低於50榮枯線，拖累歐元區
10 月綜合 PMI 由 9 月 53.1 下滑至 51.9，為四個月以來新低，經濟基本面承壓將不
利 EUR/USD上行；此外，法國社會及政經局勢不穩導致法債殖利率突破5%，市場
對其財政及政治危機擔憂持續，也將不利歐元表現。二、美國上週初請失業金人數
降至 19.4萬人，為三個月以來新低，顯示勞動力市場依然穩健，且10月 S&P製造
業 PMI為 56.0續創 53個月新高，服務業及綜合PMI維持穩健，顯示美國經濟仍具
韌性，預期將限縮 EUR/USD上行空間。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD
各天期均線維持空頭排列，且匯價持續承壓於月線之下，顯示短線空方趨勢仍未改
變，在能有效突破上方月線前仍以震盪偏空看待。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD 本週
呈現震盪偏空的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1120-1.1270

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
新台幣利率走勢分析

115.10.19-115.10.22  
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.825%  
30天期短票利率   1.67% ~ 1.68%  
  公債－10年期- A15109 2.000%~2.002% 
      － 5 年期- A15108     1.88%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，儘管市場預期強烈颱風「薔琵」來襲，週五金融市場可能
因颱風假而暫停交易，銀行間多預留帳上資金以應不時之需，但市況仍屬充
裕，即便有新增調度需求，也很容易滿足，令短率朝區間低位移動。 
週四隔夜拆款利率區間在 0.805%~0.825%， 30 天期短票主要成交在
1.65%~1.68%，利率低位較上週下跌約 1 個基本點。

二、 本週市場交投冷清，10 年期指標券 A15109 週四於等殖系統成交在 2.002%，
5 年期指標券 A15108則未成交，最近成交利率為 1.88%。 
週二 10 年期公債標售得標利率 2.001%，落於市場預期區間下緣，並創 2008
年以來新高，顯示市場持續反映經濟成長與通膨預期。 
考量通膨前景及國際債市走勢仍具不確定性，市場觀望氣氛濃厚；惟第四季公
債供給相對有限，可望抑制殖利率大幅彈升。預期短線台債殖利率維持區間整
理，5 年券及 10 年券交易區間分別估於 1.82%~1.84%及 1.98%~2.01%。

## Document 053: 2026年10月PMI数据点评：季节性因素叠加外部扰动加剧，10月制造业景气度回落至收缩区间

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年10月PMI数据点评：季节性因素叠加外部扰动加剧，10月制造业景气度回落至收缩区间.pdf
Report source: 东方金诚
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1

季节性因素叠加外部扰动加剧，10月制造业景气度回落至收缩区间 
———— 2026年 10月 PMI数据点评 
王青  冯琳 
事件：根据国家统计局公布的数据，2026年10月，中国制造业采购经理指数（PMI）为49.3%，比
9月回落0.8个百分点；10月非制造业商务活动指数为49.7%，比9月回落0.5个百分点，其中，建筑
业 PMI为48.0%，比9月回落 2.3个百分点，服务业PMI为 50.0%，比9月回落0.2个百分点；10月综
合 PMI产出指数为49.7%，比 9月回落1.0个百分点。 
10月PMI数据要点解读如下： 
10月制造业PMI较上月下行0.8个百分点，至49.3%，再度回落至收缩区间，降幅大于市场普遍预
期。背后主要有三个原因：首先是季节性因素。10月长假会导致制造业景气度季节性下行。数据显示，
过去十年中，10月制造业 PMI比 9月平均下行 0.4个百分点。其次是外部扰动加剧。近期中东局势升
温，国际油价大幅上涨，对国内石化产业链形成较大冲击。高频数据显示，10月聚氯乙烯、苯乙烯等
行业开工率大幅下行。这是 10月制造业 PMI降幅大于季节性、特别是其中的生产指数下降1.9个百分
点至49.8%的一个重要原因。最后，10月国内汽车销量下行幅度较大，居民消费偏弱，也会对制造业景
气度形成一定拖累。数据显示，10月新订单指数为49.4%，环比下降1.1个百分点。值得注意的是，此
前两个月一直处于扩张区间的新出口订单指数在 10月下降 1.3个百分点，至 48.7%，主要受国外圣诞
相关订单在上月集中释放影响。 
需要指出的是，10月安排使用地方政府债务结存限额 5500亿元，今年 8000亿元新型政策性金融
工具全面落地，以及当月地方政府专项债中用于项目建设的资金占比显著提高，稳投资政策全面发力，
加之当月出口增速仍会保持高位，都对10月制造业景气度提供一定支撑。 
最后，10月高技术制造业和装备制造业PMI分别为51.3%和 50.6%，较上月下行1.2和 0.4个百分
点，但都处于扩张区间。背后是在市场供需两旺及政策倾斜力度较大的双轮驱动下，当前这两个行业
持续处于高增长状态，抗冲击能力较强。10月消费品行业和基础原材料行业PMI分别为 48.8%和 47.3%，
较上月下行1.9和0.7个百分点，景气度回落较为明显，且都处于收缩区间。除长假后消费高峰消退、

### Page 2

2
当月基础原材料行业转向淡季等短期因素影响外，这两个行业景气度偏低，主要与当前国内消费需求
不足、房地产市场持续调整等有关。
价格方面，10月出厂价格指数和主要原材料购进价格指数分别为51.7%和 56.8%，较上月下行 2.3
和 4.0个百分点。这一方面显示 10月工业品价格环比上涨势头有所缓和——主要是在高基数背景下，
当月国际原油、国内煤炭等大宗商品价格上行幅度明显低于上月；另一方面也表明，在中东局势升温，
国内煤炭复产有待进一步推进，以及稳投资带动钢铁、水泥等需求增加影响下，10月工业品价格保持
升势。我们判断，10月PPI同比会达到5.2%左右的高位，涨幅较上月扩大0.2个百分点。不过，10月
主要原材料购进价格指数继续明显高于出厂价格指数，显示在终端需求偏弱背景下，上游原油、煤炭、
铜等大宗商品价格大幅上涨向下游传导不畅。这意味着后期中下游企业会面临更大的利润下行压力。
10月服务业PMI为50.0%，比上月回落0.2个百分点，略低于市场预期。一方面，“十一”长假带
动居民旅游出行、文化娱乐及餐饮需求集中释放，10月相关行业景气度回升明显，且均位于扩张区间。

另一方面，10月国内股市大幅调整，加之当月为季初信贷小月，政府债券融资规模环比下行，10月金
融行业 PMI回落且降幅较大，是本月服务业景气度回落的主要原因。值得注意的是，受AI投资热潮等
推动，当前互联网软件及信息技术服务等新动能行业发展较快，景气度处于55%以上较高水平，而楼市
调整导致房地产行业PMI持续处在较深收缩区间，显示服务业中的新旧动能转换也在持续推进。
10月建筑业 PMI为 48.0%，较上月大幅下行 2.3个百分点。中国物流信息中心数据显示，10月土
木工程建筑业相对稳定，商务活动指数较上月上升，显示稳投资政策发力对建筑业景气度有支撑作用。
不过，当月房屋建筑业景气水平较上月明显回落，表明房地产市场调整的拖累效应加大。总体上看，
10月建筑业 PMI再度进入收缩区间，显示各类稳投资措施还需在加快资金投放、尽快形成实物工作量
等方面加强，进而有效对冲房地产投资下滑影响，推动四季度投资止跌回稳。
整体上看，10月制造业 PMI回落至收缩区间，主要是受季节性因素、外部扰动加剧及国内有效需

求不足等因素的复合影响。往后看，伴随长假影响结束，预计11月制造业PMI有望回升到49.7%左右。
数据显示，过去十年中，11月制造业PMI环比平均回升0.2个百分点，景气度会有季节性上行。另外，
接下来稳投资政策发力显效，也会对年底前制造业景气度有较强的支撑作用。

## Document 076: 2026金融街论坛年会解读解读：筑牢流动性防线，提升制度包容性

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026金融街论坛年会解读解读：筑牢流动性防线，提升制度包容性.pdf
Report source: 国新证券股份
Date: 2026-11-01
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

市场研究部 2026年10月20日
事件
10 月 19 日，2026 年金融街论坛年会召开，央行、金融监管
总局、证监会、国家外汇管理局主要负责人分别发表演讲。
解读
一、央行行长潘功胜的主题演讲的主要政策
一是将会同金融监管总局、中国证监会设立应对非银机构大额
赎回的流动性支持工具。
二是扩大常备借贷便利（SLF）的适用机构范围。
三是推动银行间和交易所债券市场互联互通落地。
四是试点进一步便利境外金融机构在境内外汇市场开展人民币
购售业务。
五是会同科技部等 10 个部门出台《关于金融支持建设北京
（京津冀）、上海（长三角）、粤港澳大湾区国际科技创新中心的
指导意见》。
此外，今年 6月，人民银行设立了境外央行回购工具，支持境
外央行或货币当局、国际金融组织、主权财富基金，通过债券回购
方式从人民银行获取人民币流动性，便利其人民币流动性管理和人
民币资产配置。
今年初，人民银行平稳切换了数字人民币新计量框架，数字人
民币定位由现金切换为存款货币。
二、金融监管总局局长丁向群主题演讲的主要政策

切实提升监管有效性，有力防控金融风险。一是坚持风险导向
强化金融监管。二是适应实践需要健全金融法治。三是突出重点环
节提高监管能力。
加力推动改革转型，强化高质量发展金融支撑。一是不断优化
机构布局。二是大力规范市场秩序。三是持续提升金融服务适配性。
始终坚持互利共赢，持续推动金融高水平开放。一是积极拓展
金融开放新空间。二是有效服务国际经贸往来。三是推动完善全球
金融治理体系。
宏观研究
筑牢流动性防线，提升制度包容性
——2026金融街论坛年会解读解读
市场表现截至 2026.10.20
资料来源：国家统计局,国新证券整理。
分析师：郝大明

邮箱：haodaming@crsec.com.cn
证券研究报告

### Page 2

三、证监会主席吴清主题演讲的主要政策
第一，坚持稳字当头，全力维护资本市场安全稳定。
第二，突出守正创新，持续深化资本市场包容性改革。
第三，强化依法从严，大力培育规范健康的市场生态。
第四，深化务实合作，有序扩大市场高水平对外开放。
四、外管局局长朱鹤新主题演讲的主要政策
近期，在存量政策提质增效基础上，结合新形势新要求，以及
调研中发现的企业新的实际需求，拟集中推出一批增量措施，支持
企业更好参与国际合作与竞争，更好利用两个市场、两种资源。
一是推出经常项目便利化一揽子政策，更好满足外贸企业多样
化结算需求，支持贸易新业态新模式发展，促进外贸提质增效。
二是推出优化资本项目外汇管理一揽子政策，更好满足企业跨
境投融资和资金配置需求，服务“引进来”和“走出去”，拓展双
向投资合作空间。
三是推动外汇市场开放发展，扩大证券公司结售汇业务试点，
支持金融机构做好企业特别是中小企业汇率风险管理服务，提升跨
境服务能力，更好支持离在岸金融市场协同发展，助力人民币国际
化。
五、我们的解读
1、筑牢流动性防线，巩固完善稳市机制建设

维护市场稳定是资本市场健康发展的前提。人民银行行长潘功
胜宣布，将会同金融监管总局、证监会设立应对非银机构大额赎回
流动性支持工具，专门针对资管机构遭遇集中赎回、债券市场陷入
“赎回—抛售”负向循环的风险场景，通过高等级抵押品融资，切
断风险自我放大链条，补齐非银体系流动性应急保障短板，提升金
融体系抗冲击能力。这一安排与此前偏重银行体系的流动性管理形
成互补，将非银机构纳入宏观审慎管理视野，显著增强了金融体系
应对突发冲击的韧性。同时，央行也将同步推出扩大常备借贷便利
适用机构范围、推动交易所与银行间债券市场互联互通落地等配套
举措，打通跨市场资金流转渠道，优化货币市场利率调控框架，为
资本市场营造稳定适宜的货币环境。
证监会主席吴清释放了两个具有即时稳市场的信号。一是同意
首批 18只主动 ETF注册，明确将其定位为“推动基金产品创新发
展的重要举措，为市场引入更多增量资金”。在宽基 ETF 已成为
近期市场企稳重要力量的背景下，主动 ETF 的扩容意味着增量资
金入市渠道的进一步拓宽。二是强调“高度警惕境外挂钩 A股个股

的杠杆产品等新风险点”，强化外部风险输入的前置防范，体现了
监管层在开放环境下对新型风险的前瞻性关注。另外，在中长期稳
市机制方面，吴清主席明确提出要“推动进一步健全适配长期投资

### Page 3

的长周期考核机制，扩大中长期资金来源、渠道和入市方式，持续
深化公募基金改革，壮大市场稳定力量”。这意味着政策思路正从
短期的流动性干预，转向培育具有逆周期属性的制度性资本。当公
募基金、保险资金、养老金等真正以年度甚至跨年度维度评估投资
绩效时，市场的非理性波动才有望获得结构性的平抑力量。
2、强化银行保险业风险治理，夯实中长期资金根基
金融监管总局局长丁向群从机构端风险防控、行业改革、对外
开放角度，明确与资本市场改革相配套的监管导向。丁向群强调要
打好风险化解主动仗，稳妥处置重点领域风险，健全风险监测预警
与早期纠正机制，实现全部金融活动监管 “全覆盖、无例外”，
消除跨市场、跨业态监管盲区，守住不发生系统性风险底线。监管
持续强化银行保险机构的风险约束，引导保险资金等长期资金规范
参与资本市场投资，不能理解为中长期资金的收缩，而是旨在通过
净化机构生态，为资本市场投融资功能发挥筑牢中介资金基础，防
范机构风险向股票、债券市场传导溢出。另外，丁向群表示将支持
更多外资机构和长期资本来华展业兴业，鼓励外资机构发挥财富管

理、养老金融领域专业优势，有助于扩大中长期资金的来源，进一
步巩固资本市场发展的稳定性。
3、深化资本市场包容性改革，为新质生产力铺设制度通道
以资本市场服务科技创新，是本次论坛改革主线。吴清主席强
调未来将“落实落细‘两创板’改革举措”、“实施更具包容性适
应性的发行上市、并购重组等制度”、“择机发布新修订的上市公
司证券发行注册管理办法”，三项表述层层递进，核心是让那些处
于成长期、尚未盈利但具备核心技术能力的企业，能够更顺畅地获
得直接融资支持。这一监管信号与当前市场结构的变化形成了有意
味的呼应。当前科技板块尽管经历了剧烈调整，但其盈利预期并未
被系统性下调，电子行业近四周上调 2027年业绩预测的公司占比
仍居前列。调整消化的主要是前期过高的估值溢价与拥挤的交易结
构，而非产业逻辑本身。此时本次强调未来将出台更具包容性的制
度安排，实质上是在市场情绪的低位区间，为下一轮科技企业的资
本化提前铺设通道。同时，吴清主席提出“加快推动出台上市公司
监管条例”与“加力推进证券公司高质量发展行动”，则从公司治

理与中介机构两个层面，为包容性改革提供了质量约束与专业支撑，
避免包容性演变为降低标准的代名词。
4、强化投资端保护，修复市场信心锚点
信心的修复，离不开对中小投资者合法权益的实质性保障。吴
清主席在本次论坛上明确推出的四项近期举措，直指市场关切的痛
点。其中，完善违规减持处罚规则与制定发布证券市场先行赔付指
导意见尤为值得关注。前者通过细化认定标准、坚持过罚相当，遏
制利用信息优势与持股成本优势进行监管套利的行为，维护了二级
市场交易的公平性；后者则通过明确适用主体与实施程序，为受损

### Page 4

投资者提供了更为便捷高效的救济渠道。从市场反馈看，10 月 19
日上证指数收涨 0.39%，银行等低估值板块表现稳健，一定程度上
反映了政策信号对短期情绪的稳定作用。尽管单日涨幅不宜过度解
读，但监管层将“保护投资者”从原则宣示转化为可操作的制度安
排，其累积效应将逐步体现在市场风险溢价的回落之中。
5、统筹开放与安全，拓展资本市场境内外配置价值
在外部环境复杂性上升的背景下，资本市场的高水平开放并未
止步，而是呈现出更强调“可预期性”与“风险可控”的特征。国
家外汇管理局局长朱鹤新公布一批外汇领域增量改革措施，其中优
化资本项目外汇管理一揽子政策和扩大证券公司结售汇业务试点等
举措，能直接服务跨境投融资便利化，便利境外机构投资 A股，也
便利境内主体参与全球市场配置。吴清主席也提出提升 QFII 投资
运作便利度、支持境内外机构合作推出基于中国资产的指数产品，
并加快推进人民币股票交易柜台与 REITs纳入沪深港通。这些举措
的深层逻辑在于，通过降低跨境交易的制度性摩擦成本，吸引更多

以配置为导向的长期外资流入。
当前沪深 300 滚动市盈率处于历史中低位区间，全 A 市盈率
中位数较年中高点已有明显回落，中国资产在全球范围内的相对性
价比正在改善。开放举措的稳步推进，为全球投资者分享中国经济
结构转型的红利提供了更畅通的渠道，同时也在资本项下形成“有
管理的流入”格局，避免短期投机资本大进大出对市场稳定性造成
冲击。
综上，本次金融街论坛出台的政策组合，反映出监管对于未来
资本市场稳健发展的完整逻辑：以流动性支持工具守住不发生系统
性风险的底线，以包容性制度改革培育增量优质供给，以投资者保
护举措修复市场信任，以有序开放引导长期资本配置。相关政策并
非孤立的举措，而是多部门协同的系统安排。短期重在缓释市场冲
击、稳定市场预期，中长期重在完善资本市场基础制度，理顺投资
与融资的平衡关系。预计随着政策效力的逐步释放，资本市场投资
与融资相协调的功能将更为健全，其在服务“十五五”经济社会高
质量发展中的枢纽作用，也将获得更为坚实的制度支撑。
投资建议
10月 19日召开的金融街论坛年会上，央行、金融监管总局、

证监会、国家外汇管理局主要负责人集中发声，围绕流动性保障、
制度创新、风险防控、高水平开放打出政策“组合拳”，从货币环
境、机构治理、投融资改革、跨境资本管理等多个维度，为资本市
场稳预期、强韧性、促改革搭建完整政策框架，有效回应了市场关
切，为下一阶段资本市场稳健发展提供了清晰的政策支撑。
风险提示
1、地缘政治恶化超预期；2、欧美经济衰退超预期。

contributions as shipment volume scales. These opportunities should complement
steady growth across Sinbon's established businesses.
Implications — We believe the margin implications of a rising semi sales mix are well
understood by the market and leave our investment thesis intact. Our constructive
view is based on steady growth in existing businesses, supplemented by medium- to
long-term opportunities in humanoid robots, drones, and LEO satellites. We also

view Sinbon as a relatively defensive stock amid AI-related market volatility, given
its limited direct AI exposure and diversified business portfolio. The shares could
benefit from investor rotation toward companies with resilient underlying
fundamentals, while emerging applications provide additional support for the
longer-term growth outlook.

Sinbon
Valuation
Our TP for Sinbon is NT$370. We value Sinbon on PE to capture the company’s earnings growth prospects. We set our target
PE at 23x, which we set at a 20% premium to the historical average of 19x since 2018. We believe 23x is justified in view of
improving fundamentals with strong momentum of industrials segment, driving a clear margin expansion and solid earnings
growth in 2026E. Applying this to our 2H26-1H27E EPS forecast, we arrive at our target price of NT$370.

Risks
Key downside risks that could cause Sinbon's share to deviate from our target price include 1) slower-than-expected growth in
non-IT/non-consumer applications; 2) Intensified competition; 3) Raw material price hike and labor cost increases; 4) FX
volatility; 5) Greater-than-expected impact from the geopolitical conflict.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

buy recommendation and a Catalyst Watch/STV Downside call corresponds to a sell recommendation. Any stock not assigned
to a Catalyst Watch Upside, Catalyst Watch Downside, STV Upside, or STV Downside call is considered Catalyst Watch/STV
Prepared for Kevin Lu

not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Chroma Ate Inc.  | Asia Pacific
Momentum Seen Sustained into
2027
SL T, photonics (transceiver+CPO), and power testing should
continue to drive growth into 2027, with contributions from FT
handlers and burn-in ovens likely emerging as well. Stay OW.
3Q26 miss on GM: See details in Results Summary
Growth momentum seen sustained into 4Q26 and 2027: Management indicated
that the strong business momentum observed in 3Q26 is likely to stay intact going

forward, driven by both power testing and semi & photonics businesses. Normalized
GM remains at ~60% (2026 guidance of ~60% GM implies GM back to ~60% in
4Q26 vs. 57.5% in 3Q26).
• Power testing: 60-70% of revenue is AI-related for 2026, and it is expected
to be sustained into 2027. HVDC testing tools could be the major driver, and
it has already received orders from customers.
• SLT: 9M26 revenue doubled on a YoY basis. SL T demand used to focus on

one major customer, but management indicated that it has added AVGO and
MRVL as new customers. Moreover, demand in 4Q26/1H27 should be mostly
related to inference applications, including CPU/ASIC/LPU.
• Photonics: 9M26 revenue grew by 4x on  a YoY basis. Demand from
transceivers should remain solid and CPO should grow notably in 2027.
Chroma focuses on Insertion 3/4o and shipments for the mass production
line next year  are seen reaching 4-5x vs. that for the pilot line this year.

Moreover, it has added another customer for a pilot run in 9M27 and mass
production in 4Q27. At the industry level, if insertion is excluded from mass
production, Insertion 3 tool demand should be higher. Opportunities will
likely be much larger when CPO penetrates the scale-up network.
• New businesses: FT handlers have been qualified by multiple HPC/CSP
customers and orders have been placed. Burn-in ovens have secured the first
CSP customer, with more HPC customers expected to follow next year.

These should be new addressable markets for Chroma in the coming years.
Stay OW: We believe Chroma should continue to benefit from the AI compute/
networking/power testing opportunities due to its strong customer relationship,
solid execution and outstanding technology expertise. Our PT of NT$2,800 is based
on 40x 2027e P/E, implying a PEG of 0.6 (vs. 1.0 over the past decade).

Results Summary
3Q26 miss on GM:
Off revenue of  NT$17,926mn (+32% QoQ/+180% YoY), GM contracted 3ppt QoQ, to
57.5%, below our estimate at 60.5% and consensus at 62.7%, mainly owing to a less
favorable product mix.
Opex of NT$3,141mn (opex ratio 17.5%) was lower than our estimate at NT$3,797mn (opex
ratio 21.2%) and consensus at NT$3,321mn (opex ratio 21.7%), reflecting operating
leverage.
This led to operating profit of NT$7,167mn (OPM up 1.2ppt QoQ, to 40.0%), 2% higher

than our estimate at NT$7,040mn (OPM 39.3%) and 14% higher than consensus at NT
$6,281mn (OPM 41.0%).
With a narrower non-op gain of NT$186mn, net profit reached NT$5,647mn (EPS NT
$13.37), 2% short of our estimate at NT$5,790mn (EPS NT$13.71) and 6% short of
consensus at NT$5,317mn (EPS NT$12.59).

A Closer Look
Test instrument and ATS segment looks set to reach another
record high in 2026
Revenue for the segment increased 55% YoY to NT$10,546mn (37% of the mix) in 2025.
We estimate it will rise 118% YoY to NT$24,564mn (40% of the mix) in 2026. The ever-
increasing power consumption of AI clusters should continue to drive demand for higher
spec testing tools. Along with our forecast of a 75% CAGR for global power supply unit

demand in the coming three years (see our report), this should remain a major driver for
Chroma, in our view.

Multiple drivers in the semiconductor business
System-level testing  (SL T) business
SL T should benefit from the capex cycle of NVIDIA's Rubin generation GPU, AMD's MI450
GPU and more ASIC accelerators.  Management pointed out that discrete testing tools are
still preferred  by major customers.  We are also seeing more HPC chip designers adopting

the system level testing process to make sure defects won't be incorporated into the AI
systems. We believe Chroma, as the first mover in this market, should benefit from
increasing chip volume, as well as higher TDP.
Metrology business
The metrology business should continue to benefit from current advanced packaging
architectures from its foundry and OSAT customers. It is confident that the performance
of its tools can win future architecture projects, which our industry checks suggest should

have higher metrology density.

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
Exhibit 4: Chroma –  Offerings for Metrology in Advanced Packaging
Source: Chroma
Chroma's metrology equipment employs white light interferometry technology. The basic
working principle is that white light is emitted on the reference mirror and the sample,
which are placed at the same distance from the light source. The reflected beams from
the reference mirror and sample surface are then received by a light sensor.
Interference occurs when two light beams collide, which could cause  them to either

strengthen or weaken, depending on the phase of the two light beams. Thus, by
measuring the intensity of the combined light beams, the distance between light sensor
and multiple points on the target surface can be calculated through an algorithm. This can
then be used to measure the dimensions of the structure or the alignment of layers.
Exhibit 5: Concept of Light Interference
Source: Keyence
Exhibit 6: Setup of Interferometer
Source: Wikipedia
CPO contribution to start from 2026

Chroma has been qualified for optical engine testing in Insertion 3 and system-level
testing in Insertion 4, and there might be incremental opportunities if supply chain players'
progress falls behind for testing equipment in Insertion 2. Over the longer term, Chroma
also  aspires to participate in the PIC optical/electrical/reliability testing in Insertion 1.

FT handlers to add another revenue stream
In HPC testing, Chroma used to focus mostly on the SL T side, although it did unveil its first
HPC FT handler earlier this year and is now going through the qualification process. We
believe this is a natural extension of its expertise and customer relationships accumulated
in SL Ts. We expect Chroma to gain more traction in FT handlers for AI HPCs in 2027.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Chroma Ate Inc. (2360.TW)
Base case, 40x 2027e P/E. We use P/E as our valuation methodology because we think it better
captures Chroma's earnings growth momentum. The stock traded at an average valuation of
~19x P/E during 2014-25 with an operating profit CAGR of 20%, implying a PEG of 1.0. We think
our target multiple of 40x is justified by the 68% operating profit CAGR we project for

2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected macro conditions
n Stronger semi and photonics and power testing demand
n Margin improvement via better product mix
n Better opex control
Risks to Downside
n Macro slowdown
n Softer-than-expected AI capex
n Slower progress in the advanced packaging capacity expansion
n Weaker consumer electronics and automobile related demand
n Margin contraction arising from lack of revenue scale and increasing opex

Structural OPM upward trend — Management expects 4Q26 to mark the 
beginning of a structural improvement in OPM, supported by rapidly scaling data 
center ASIC revenue and operating leverage. Management indicated that the OPM 
could exceed 20% in 2027. Meanwhile, selective pricing adjustments across 
traditional businesses should help offset rising supply chain costs. Beyond data 
centers, Smart Edge should benefit from new AI PCs, AI tablets, automotive 
platforms and networking applications, including continued collaboration with 
NVIDIA on next-generation AI computing products. While smartphone demand 
remains relatively subdued, management expects the expanding AI business to 
increasingly dominate MediaTek’s overall growth and earnings trajectory.
Second-Generation ASIC on Track for 1Q28 
Production, with a Second Customer 
Management expressed increasing confidence in achieving the upper end of its 15–
20% market share target for the estimated US$80bn data center AI accelerator 
ASIC market in 2027, implying approximately US$16bn in potential revenue. This 
represents a substantial increase from the revised US$2.5bn target for 2026, 
supported by strong demand visibility and continued supply chain expansion. 
Management emphasized that securing adequate foundry, packaging and other 
critical component capacity remains essential to meeting customer requirements. 
Importantly, the revenue opportunity reflects MediaT ek’s increasing participation in 
the value chain, encompassing advanced process technologies, packaging, high-
speed interconnects and custom silicon integration. Despite recent market 
concerns over AI infrastructure financing and CSP capital expenditure 
sustainability, management remains confident that AI compute demand will 
continue growing over the next several years, particularly for customized 
accelerators offering superior performance per watt and total cost of ownership.
Figure 1. Mediatek - 3Q26 results overview

Management reiterated that its second AI accelerator project remains on schedule 
for high-volume production in 1Q28, despite market concerns regarding design 
complexity, advanced packaging yields and potential delays. The new accelerator is 
expected to deliver multiple times the performance per watt of the first-generation 
product, significantly improving customer TCO and strengthening Media T ek’s 
competitive positioning . Management acknowledged manufacturing challenges 
associated with its U.S. IDM packaging partner but highlighted progress in 
identifying key yield constraints and implementing corrective measures, expressing 
high confidence in further yield improvements. Importantly, Media T ek also 
confirmed a separate design win with a second, non-Chinese data center

Mediatek (2454.TW)

customer, with initial revenue expected in 2028. The company is already
collaborating with its existing customer on subsequent-generation products,
although specifications are still being finalized. Management expects another year
of strong revenue growth in 2028 but declined to provide specific revenue guidance
or customer contribution estimates.
Expanding competitive advantages through advanced node

design/packaging, SerDes and NVIDIA NVLink Fusion
MediaT ek continues investing in advanced technology capabilities, including 448G
SerDes, advanced packaging, HBM integration and co-packaged optics (CPO), to
establish a competitive advantage in increasingly complex AI infrastructure
designs. Management emphasized that its differentiation comes not from
individual technologies but from integrating them into high-performance, power-
efficient and commercially viable solutions. More importantly, its partnership with

NVIDIA through NVLink Fusion enables customers to integrate custom XPUs into
NVIDIA’s rack-scale ecosystem, while its support for UALink and Ethernet-based
architectures provides greater deployment flexibility. MediaT ek is expanding
beyond individual silicon components toward subsystems and rack-level
solutions, shortening customers’ development cycles and accelerating time to
revenue. Management indicated encouraging engagement with multiple potential

customers and expects broader adoption opportunities. The 448G SerDes
development is progressing well and is expected to support both existing
accelerator programs and future customers, with management not anticipating it
to become a critical schedule bottleneck.
Structural OPM expansion trend; Reiterate Buy
Management expects 4Q26 to mark the beginning of a structural improvement in
operating profitability, supported by rapidly scaling data center ASIC revenue and

operating leverage. Although ASIC gross margins are expected to remain below
MediaT ek’s traditional corporate average, the significantly larger revenue
contribution and more favorable operating expense leverage should drive
meaningful operating margin expansion. Management indicated that the operating
margin could exceed 20% in 2027, potentially somewhat higher, compared with its
historical mid-teen level. Meanwhile, selective pricing adjustments across

traditional businesses should help offset rising supply chain costs, although these
increases are primarily intended to preserve profitability rather than expand gross
margins. Beyond data centers, Smart Edge should benefit from new AI PCs, AI
tablets, automotive platforms and networking applications, including continued
collaboration with NVIDIA on next-generation AI computing products. While
smartphone demand remains relatively subdued, management expects the

expanding AI business to increasingly dominate MediaT ek’s overall growth and
earnings trajectory.
Given MediaT ek’s better operating leverage, we lift our earnings projection for
2027 /2028 5%/5%, mainly reflecting better OPM. We maintain our Buy rating with
an unchanged TP of NT$6,800, implying 29x our 2027 /28 EPS estimates.

### Page 9

Mediatek (2454.TW)
30 October 2026 Citi Research
9
Mediatek
Company description
Mediatek (MTK) is a fabless IC company that designs SoC for smartphone,
smart TV, and digital media devices. It also develops chips for connectivity,
networking, smart home gadgets, and automotive sectors. It was founded in
1997, is listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange, and is headquartered in Taiwan.
It has sales and research subsidiaries in mainland China, Singapore, India,

the US, Japan, Korea, Denmark, and England. It primarily sells its products in
China and other emerging-market countries.
Investment strategy
We rate Mediatek shares as Buy. We expect Mediatek to ride on 5G migration
in emerging markets and market share gain of its high-end Dimensity series in
China market as well. In particular, we see strong growth potential from its AI
ASIC, NTN-satellite business in the long term. We believe solid fundamentals

and strong operating leverage will support Mediatek’s margins outlook.
Valuation
Our NT$6,800 target price (rounded) for Mediatek is based on 29x P/E to
2027 /2028 EPS, which is 1-std above its five-year average forward P/E given
the promising outlook from its AI ASIC business starting from 2H26. We
believe our target PE is also justified by Mediatek's global and regional peers'
trading at an average of c32x PER. AI ASIC development with CSPs should

provide more upside from 2027 onwards. We also see Mediatek as well
prepared for 6G and 6G+NTN Satellite communication. Despite lukewarm
smartphone overall deamand, we expect Mediatek to benefit from 5G
migration in emerging markets and its Dimensity series should also gain
shares in flagship models. Non-smartphone business is also seeing
stabilizing TV and Io T demand with Mediatek outgrowing the overall market
on its better technology in WiFi 7 and connectivity.

Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) weaker-than-expected growth from AI ASIC business; 2)
more severe competition in the smartphone segment; 3) slower overall
demand related to macroeconomic risks, especially for smartphone demand;
and 4) further price hikes from foundries.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment
rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered

stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating
and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also

suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price
chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in

exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the
expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility

or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be
different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

信邦(3023 TT)  
台灣股市︱電子中游 
投 資 速 報 
買進-首次 目標價 NT$373 (潛在報酬+35.6%) 2026-10-28

半導體設備連接線需求 強勁  
◼ 結 論 與 建 議 
基 於 以 下 理 由 ， 研 究 部 給 予 信 邦 逢 低 買 進 評 等  
1. 1~3 Q26 營 收 達 271.83 億 元 ， 年 增 15.24% ， 3~9 月 營 收 連 增 7 個月 ，
表 現 優 於 市 場 預 期 。  
2. 工 業 （ 半 導 體 設 備 、 自 動 化 ） 、 醫 療 及 車 用 連 接 線 為 三 大 長 期 成 長 引 擎 。  
3. 公 司 半 導 體 設 備 從 線 束 升 級 至 機 櫃 組 裝 大 幅 提 升 附 加 價 值 與 能 見 度；汽 車
業 務 受 惠 電 動 化 與 控 制 模 組 放 量；醫 療 則 受 惠 中 國 市 場 回 溫 可 提 供 近 兩
年 明 確 動 能，人 形 機 器 人 則 為 2028 年 後 的 爆 發 潛 力， 預估 2027 年 EPS  
17.78 元 PE 約 15. 5X， 建 議 買 進 ， 目 標 價 為 373 元 (21* 202 7 年 E P S )。

◼ 重點摘要  
公 司 簡 介  
公司是台灣領先的電子零件整合設計與製造服務商，專注於客製化線束
（ Cable Assembly ） 、 精 密 連 接 器 組 件 及 系 統 整 合 解 決 方 案 。 核 心 業 務 以 提
供從設計、打樣、製造到組裝的完整解決方案。產品以連接線組為主（約
78% ） ， 連 接 器 及 零 組 件 約22% 。 代 理 Hirose 等 國 際 大 廠 連 接 器 。 客 戶 涵 蓋
全球前十大風機廠商中的 8 家、前 十 大 醫 療 成 像 設 備 商 中 的 6 家、前 二 十 大
工業設備商中的 9 家、前十大汽車商中的 6 家等。 2025 年產品應用占比大
致為工業約 31% 、通訊 24% 、 綠 能 20% 、 汽 車 / 航太 16% 、 醫 療 9% （實際
會 隨 年 度 波 動 ） 。 銷 售 地 區 以 中 國 大 陸 為 主（ 約53% ） ， 其 次 為 美 國 、 台 灣 、
歐洲等。積極擴充半導體設備機櫃組裝產能及商用無人機相關產品。

2

全球半導體設備市場規模快速成長  
根 據 市 調 機 構 數 據 顯 示 ， 全 球 前 段 製 程 設 備 （ Waf er Fa b Equipmen t, 
WFE ）， 2025 年 銷 售 額 創 下 1,169 億 美 元 新 高 ， 預 估 2026 年將再成長
23.1% ，達 1,439 億 美 元 。 此 數 字 較 SEMI 先 前 於 2025 年 底 所 公 布 的 預 測
大 幅 上 修 ， 主 要 受 惠 於 先 進 記 憶 體 、 尤 其 是 HBM 相關 DRAM 技 術 ， 以 及
先 進 邏 輯 應 用 投 資 持 續 增 加 。 隨 著 製 造 商 因 應 AI 應 用 需 求 ， 加 速 擴 充 產 能
及 推 進 技 術 升 級，預 估 2027 年及 2028 年 將 分 別 成 長 21.8% 及 14.1% ，2028
年 市 場 規 模 可 望 突 破 2,000 億 美 元 。  
圖 二 、 全 球 半 導 體 設 備 市 場 預 測

資 料 來 源 ： SEMI 、 康 和 投 顧 整 理 ， 2026/10

工業應用為 最 強 成 長 動 能  
1~3Q26 營 收 占 比 37% ， YOY+38.9% ， 主 要 受 惠 於 全 球 半 導 體 設 備 需 求 擴
張 及 智 能 製 造 自 動 化 升 級 潮 。 內 部 結 構 佔 比 包括 倉 儲 物 流 自 動 化 近 40% 、
半 導 體 設 備 佔 30 ~35% 、 運 輸 / 建設 設 備 線 束 佔 10% 、 能 源 管 理 類 佔 1 0% 。
動能 主 要 來 自 半 導 體 設 備 機 內 / 機 外 訊 號 與 電 源 線 束 組 裝 以 及 倉 儲 物 流 自
動 化 需 求 增 加 ， 27F 營收 YOY+20% 。  
a. 半 導 體 設 備 : 為 最 大 亮 點 ， 從 傳 統 線 束 升 級 至 整 機 機 櫃 組 裝 與 系 統 整 合 。
提 供 機 內 ／ 機 外 訊 號 線 與 電 源 線，並 開 始 整 合 其 他 零 組 件 做 成 小 型 機 櫃
／ 模 組 給 客 戶 。  
✓ 供 應 鏈 地 位 ： 公司 為 全 球 半 導 體 前 段 與 後 段 設 備 大 廠 （ 如  AS ML 、應
用 材 料 等 ） 的 重 要 高 度 客 製 化 線 束 及 系 統 整 合 供 應 商 。  
✓ 應 用 領 域 ： 先 進 節 點 製 造 中 的 光 刻 （ 微 影 ） 、 蝕 刻 和 沉 積 設 備 、 精密
製 程 設 備 中 的 線 束 系 統 、 機 櫃 半 成 品 組 裝 。  
✓ 訂 單 與 產 能 狀 況： 受 產 品 銷 量 增 長 、 複 雜 性 提 升 及 新 客 戶 訂 單 需 求 驅
動，公 司 目 前 通 過 加 班 和 增 加 第 二 班 次 滿 足 產 能 需 求 ；為 進 一 步 擴
充 產 能 ， 斥 資 逾 50 億 元 在 苗 栗 銅 鑼 科 學 園 區 廠 房 擴 建 產 能 ， 主 要
目 的 即 是 用 於 擴 充 半 導 體 設 備 高 階 模 組 與 線 束 生 產 量 能 。 計 劃 於
2Q27 年 正 式 投 入 運 營 ， 預計 增 長 趨 勢 將 持 續 到 2030 年 。

本公司不負任何法律責任，請投資人自行斟酌參考 
3

✓ 液 冷 散 熱 技 術 延 伸 : 已 展 示 四 款 數 據 中 心 熱 管 理 解 決 方 案 原 型（ 兩 款 浸
沒 式 液 冷 、 兩 款 傳 統 液 冷 ） ， 可為 OD M 和 CSP 提 供 客 製 化 熱 管
理 服 務 ， 產品預計 2026 年 下 半 年 開 始 送 樣 ， 2H27 年 對 盈 利 產 生
實 質 性 貢 獻 。  
b. 人 形 機 器 人 ： 機 器 人 線 束 預 估 4Q26 起進 入 量 產 ， 已 取 得 5 家美系 Tier 1
獨 家 訂 單 ， 提 供 整 組 線 束 模 組 （ 獨 家 供 應 ） ， 終 端 應 用 多 在 工 廠 或 倉 儲
物 流 。 預 估 營 收 占 比 2026 年約 1% 、 2027 年約 2% 、 2028 年 有 望 達
13% 。 毛 利 率 預 估 約 40% ， 2028 年 有 機 會 達 10 萬 套 以 上 。  
✓ 產品 : 提 供 完 整 線 纜 模 組 （ 感 測 器 、 連 接 器 、 訊 號 傳 輸 線 、 充 電 線 、 關
節 線 束 等 ） ，涵 蓋 全 身 應 用，單 台 機 器 人 線 束 產 值 約 1,000 – 1,500
美 元 （ 視 整 合 程 度 ， 一 台 可 能 需 要 50 – 150 條 線 束 ） 。  
✓ 技 術 門 檻 高：關 節 線 束 需 通 過 極 高 次 數（ 數 十 萬 至 百 萬 次 ）彎 折 測 試 ，
具 備 耐 彎、耐 壓、防 水、輕 量 化 等 特 性，屬 客 製 化 總 解 決 方 案 （Tota l 
Solution ） ， 多 數 專 案 為 獨 家 供 應 （ Only S our ce ） 。

2)  買進 / Bu y： 我 們 對 該 評 價 個 股 未 來 展 望 正 向 ， 並 預 估 於 未 來 12 個 月 投 資 期 間 ， 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 大 於 25%

4)  未評等 / N ot Ra te d ： 至 目 前 我 們 沒 有 足 夠 基 本 資 料 判 斷 該 公 司 評 等 ； 或 此 期 間 康 和 與 該 公 司 有 特 定 交 易 ， 基 於 規 範 或 康
和 政 策 ， 限 制 予 該 公 司 評 等

免責宣言 :  
本 投 資 報 告 內 容 屬 康 和 投 資 顧 問 股 份 有 限 公 司 版 權 所 有 ， 禁 止 任 何 形 式 之 抄 襲 、 引 用 或 轉 載 。 本 研 究 報 告 所 載 內 容 ， 純 屬
研 究 性 質 ， 所 載 述 意 見 可 隨 時 予 以 更 改 或 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 ， 僅 提 供 康 和 集 團 內 部 同 仁 及 客 戶 參 考 ， 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何
意 見 ， 不 構 成 ( 且 不 應 解 釋 為 ) 在 任 何 司 法 管 轄 區 內 ， 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 劵 或 其 他 投 資 標 的 之 要 約 、 招 攬 、 邀 請 、 宣 傳 、 誘 使 ，
或 任 何 不 論 種 類 或 形 式 之 表 示 、 建 議 或 推 薦 買 賣 本 研 究 報 告 所 述 的 任 何 證 劵 。 報 告 內 容 所 提 及 之 各 項 業 務 、 財 務 等 相 關 資
訊 、 意 見 及 預 估 ， 係 取 材 自 據 信 為 可 靠 之 資 料 來 源 ， 既 不 以 明 示 或 默 示 的 方 式 ， 對 資 料 之 準 確 性 、 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 做 出 任
何 陳 述 或 保 證 ， 且 報 告 係 基 於 特 定 日 期 所 做 之 判 斷 ， 有 其 時 效 性 限 制 ， 如 有 變 更 ， 本 公 司 將 不 做 預 告 或 更 新 。 對 於 本 投 資
報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 劵 、 投 資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 於 決 策 時 應 審 慎 衡 量 本 身 風 險 ， 並 就
投 資 結 果 自 行 負 責 。

個百分點，反映高毛利授權收入佔比提升及營收規模擴大，帶動稅前淨利
42.9 億元(季增 8.6%/年增 27.4%)，優於我們/市場預期 7%/5%，推估 EPS 
12.55 元。展望 4Q26，9 月單月授權收入提升至 17.5 億元(vs 2025 年底 14
億元)，隨各國業務推進，估遊戲授權營收年增 33%，帶動整體營收年增
19.9%至 68.3 億元，預估 EPS 12.56 元(季持平/年增 18%)，上修 4%。 
美國跨州加速推進，估 2026/27 年遊戲授權年增 32%/15% 
網路遊戲授權為鈊象主要獲利成長引擎，預期 2026/27 年授權收入年增
32%/15%。以 1-8M26 區域別而言，1) 美國營收年增 65%、占比 14%，目
前全美共 35 個州可合法經營 Sweepstakes 相關業務，鈊象於深耕之三大州
市占率約 60%，過去三年營收表現平穩，近期透過新型 Social SS 拓展至其
他 32 州，將為未來數年最主要成長動能； 2) 東南亞營收年增 18%、占比
54%，雖基期已高，但考量市佔率低且部分地區仍有新增合作機會，預期維
持穩健成長；3) 其他地區營收年增 86%、占比 11%，南美洲近一年表現亮
眼、非洲自去年底進入市場後亦逐步取得進展、歐洲雖已取得多國執照且營
收持續成長，惟受國際大廠競爭、品牌知名度影響拓展速度相對較慢。 
海外授權多點開花，維持買進評等 
看好隨美國業務跨州加速推進、東南亞市場穩健成長，及 TaDa Gaming 品
牌在歐洲及其他新興市場持續擴張，帶動鈊象 2026/27 年營收年增 19%/10%；
隨高毛利遊戲授權業務佔比提升至 76%/79%、營運槓桿發酵，估營業利益
率年增 1.1 個/1.5 個百分點，EPS 年增 24.3%/11.5%至 47.88 元/53.38 元，
上修 3%/1%，評價維持歷史區間中值 20 倍本益比，以 2H26-1H27 EPS 
50.85 元(前次為 2026 年)，推得目標價 1,000 元，給予買進。 
元大觀點 
⧫

27%，優於預期 7%；4Q26 授權收入持續疊加，估營收年增 20%。 
⧫

歐洲及新興市場持續擴張，估 2026/27 年授權收入年增 32%/15%。

營運分析  
3Q26 網路遊戲授權營收年增 43%，自結稅前淨利年增 27%，優於預期 7% 
鈊象 3Q26 營收 67.5 億元(季增 5.2%/年增 25.3%)，達成我們/市場預期 103%/101%，略優於預期，
營收佔比為網路遊戲授權 77%、網路遊戲 APP 21% 、商用遊戲機 2%，暑假遊戲旺季，且世界盃
投注熱潮外溢至其他線上博弈項目，帶動網路遊戲授權營收季增 8%/年增 43%；自結營業利益 41.6
億元(季增 8.5%/年增 34.3%)，營業利益率季增 1.9 個百分點至 61.7%，優於我們預期 1.8 個百分
點，反映高毛利授權收入佔比提升及營收規模擴大，自結稅前淨利 42.9 億元(季增 8.6%/年增
27.4%)，優於我們/市場預期 7%/5%，推估 EPS 12.55 元。 
三大業務 1-9M26 狀況如下： 
➢ 網路遊戲授權(營收佔比 77%)：累計營收年增 32%，美國及其他地區成長快速、東南亞穩健
擴張，單月遊戲授權收入提升至 17-18 億元，相對 2025 年底約 14 億元成長。 
➢ 網路遊戲 APP (21%) ：累計營收年減 6%，台灣市場已接近飽和及競爭激烈，且觀察 APP 玩
家付費率(ARPU)近年來維持在 1.5%至 2%之間，顯示休閒玩家的付費意願已達極限。 
➢ 商用遊戲機(2%)：累計營收年減 26%，營收佔比下滑，中國內需疲弱、競爭者新品推出快速，
且部分遊樂場停止經營使中古機台流入市場，壓抑新機需求。

授權收入持續疊加，估 4Q26 營收年增 20% 
9 月底鈊象單月遊戲授權收入提升至逾 17.5 億元(vs 2025 年底 14 億元)，隨公司於各國業務持續推
進，授權收入有望進一步擴張，估 4Q26 網路遊戲授權營收年增 33%至 53 億元，帶動整體營收年
增 19.9%至 68.3 億元，上修 2%，預估營業利益 42.1 億元(季增 1.1%/年增 24.5%)、稅後淨利 35.4
億元(季持平/年增 18%)，估 EPS 12.56 元，上修 4%。

海外版圖持續擴張，估 2026/27 年網路遊戲授權營收年增 32%/15% 
網路遊戲授權為鈊象主要獲利成長引擎，1-9M26 網路遊戲授權營收 145 億元，年增 32%，營收占
比自 2025 年之 68%提升至 75%，於遊戲授權業務毛利率達 100%，進一步推動公司毛利率提升。 
以 1-8M26 區域別而言， 1) 美國營收年增 65%、占比 14%，目前全美共  35 個州可合法經營
Sweepstakes 相關業務，鈊象於深耕之三大州市占率約 60%，過去三年營收表現平穩，近期透過新
型 Social SS 拓展至其他 32 州，將為未來數年最主要成長動能，同時我們認為於美國更長遠成長
引擎，將來自跨足市場規模數倍於 SS 領域的 online casino 市場；2) 東南亞營收年增 18%、占比
維持54%，雖基期已高，但考量市佔率低且部分地區仍有新增合作機會，預期維持穩健成長； 3) 其
他地區(排除台灣、中國、美國、東南亞)營收年增 86%、占比 11%，以 TaDa Gaming 品牌業務為
主，其中南美洲近一年表現亮眼、非洲自去年底進入市場後亦逐步取得進展、歐洲為全球最大線上
博弈市場，雖已取得多國執照且營收持續成長，惟受國際大廠競爭、品牌知名度影響拓展速度相對
較慢，公司正積極擴大代理商合作以加速產品上架排程。 
整體而言，隨美國業務跨州加速推進、東南亞市場穩健貢獻，及 TaDa Gaming 品牌在歐洲及其他
新興市場持續擴張，預期鈊象 2026/27 年網路授權遊戲收入 198 億/229 億元，年增 32%/15%。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：軟體 2026 年 10 月 22 日 IGS 第5 頁，共 15頁  
產業概況  
全球線上博弈市場規模將以 CAGR 10.5%成長至 2034 年 2,864 億美元 
根據 Facts and Factors 2026/2月報告， 2025 年全球線上博弈市場規模約 1,055 億美元，預計將以
CAGR 10.5% 擴張至 2034 年達 2,864 億美元，動能包括多國政府積極推動線上博弈合法化，以爭
取稅收增長、行動裝置及 5G 網路普及、數位支付機制成熟，以及 AI 技術在玩家行為分析與精準行
銷上的廣泛應用。按照遊戲類型劃分，體育博弈佔比最高達 48%、其次為線上賭場；按照區域劃分，
歐洲佔 41%為最大的線上博弈市場，歸因於多國具備極為完善且透明的監管體系、北美多州逐步開
放線上博弈，帶動市場成長、拉丁美洲與雅泰地區因基期低及滲透率提升，預期將快速成長。  
圖 7：2021-2034 年全球線上博弈市場規模預測

資料來源：Global market insight、元大投顧 
社交博弈不涉及現金提現，純娛樂性質降低法規監管門檻 
社交博弈不同於傳統線上博弈，其核心在於不涉及現金提現的純娛樂性質，主要受網路遊戲法規監
管，根據 The Business Research Company《2026 年社交博弈全球市場報告》，2025 年全球社交
博弈市場規模為 92.4 億美元，預計將以 CAGR 9.1% 持續成長，至 2030 年達 144 億美元，動能來
自全球網路普及率提升進而擴大博弈遊戲的受眾，此外業者積極開發創新的技術平台與互動機制，
有效提升社交博弈市場的獲利。在區域表現方面，北美為全球最大社交博弈市場，亞太地區受惠於
人口紅利與網路設備普及，預期將成為成長最快速的區域。

### Page 6

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：軟體 2026 年 10 月 22 日 IGS 第6 頁，共 15頁  
全球遊戲市場 2025-2028 年 CAGR 達 4.9%，PC 遊戲成長最強勁 
根據 Newzoo 2025/12 遊戲產業市場報告，2025 年全球遊戲市場總收入預計達 1,967 億美元，年增 
7.5%，成長動能來自既有玩家更強的付費深度與更長的時間投入。細分平台市場，手機遊戲 (佔比
55%)年增 7.7%，受益於成熟的線上服務與廣大的全球覆蓋率、PC 遊戲(22%)年增 10.4%，受惠於
高品質遊戲的密集發行，以及 Steam 平台在亞洲市場滲透率提升、主機遊戲(23%)年增 4.2%，受
惠硬體設備更替及指標性作品推出。 
展望未來，預估全球遊戲產業 2025-2028 年將以 4.9%的 CAGR 成長至 2,272 億美元，並以 PC 遊
戲的 6.6%的 CAGR 表現最為強勁，其市場規模有望於 2028 年超越主機遊戲，主要受惠 Steam 降
低遊戲開發及供給門檻、東亞與新興市場玩家數量增加，以及於數位原生代 (Z 世代和 Alpha 世代)
成為消費主力。 
圖 9：2022-2028 年全球遊戲市場規模預測

資料來源：Newzoo、元大投顧 
台灣遊戲市場維持成長趨勢，但付費意願下滑 
根據資誠《2023-2027 全球娛樂暨媒體業展望》報告指出， 2024 年台灣電玩遊戲與電競產業產值
約達 39 億美元，2022 年至 2027 年 CAGR 達 4.8%，至 2027 年產值達 44.6 億美元，主要動能來
自 1) 智慧型手機滲透率高；2) 高速數據傳輸方案價格親民；3) 5G 覆蓋率持續提升等。在付費狀況
方面，台灣在全球行動遊戲營收中，每使用者平均收入 (ARPU)長期位居全球前十名，根據資策會
統計，台灣網友遊玩數位遊戲的滲透率已高達 81.4%，但受疫後消費回歸線下、通膨及影音社群等
多元娛樂排擠資源影響，2025 年整體玩家具付費習慣的比例下降至 35.5%，較前一年下滑約一成，
反應消費者對免費內容的依賴度提升，在付費決策上更趨於精打細算，未來市場的獲利動能在於透
過精準的數據分析與高黏著度的內容經營，深挖核心付費族群的長期價值。

獲利調整與股票評價  
遊戲授權業務多點開花，給予鈊象買進評等，目標價 1,000,元 
展望 2026/27 年，預估鈊象營收年增 19%/10%至 263 億/290 億元，主要動能來自美國業務跨州加
速推進、東南亞市場穩健貢獻，及 TaDa Gaming 品牌在歐洲及其他新興市場持續擴張，估帶動網
路遊戲授權營收年增 32%/15%，網路遊戲 app 則因台灣市場已接近飽和及競爭激烈、付費率見頂，
預估營收年減 7%/5%，商用遊戲機受中國消費力衰退及美國關稅不利因素影響，預估營收年減
28%/年持平；隨高毛利遊戲授權業務佔比提升至 76%/79%、營運槓桿發酵，預估帶動營業利益率
提升年增 1.1 個/1.5 個百分點至 60.4%/61.9%，使營業利益年增 21%/13%至 159 億/179 億元、稅
後淨利年增 24%/12%至 134 億/150 億元，估 EPS 47.88 元/53.38 元，上修 3%/1%。 
鈊象歷史三年交易於 10-30 倍本益比，看好公司海外遊戲授權業務保持擴張態勢，全球線上博弈市
場規模 2025 至 2034 年將以 CAGR 10.5% 成長，鈊象於既有市場擴大市佔，並持續開拓新市場，
帶動獲利逐年走升，此次評價維持歷史區間中值 20 倍本益比，以 2H26-1H27 EPS 50.85 元(前次
為 2026 年)，推得目標價 1,000 元，給予買進評等。

### Page 11

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：軟體 2026 年 10 月 22 日 IGS 第11 頁，共 15頁

公司簡介  
鈊象成立於 1989 年，為台灣商用遊戲機與網路遊戲大廠，專注於遊戲軟硬體之設計、研發、製造與行銷，初期以發展商用
遊戲機為主，擁有出色的 IP 如《三國戰記》和《西遊》等。然而為分散中國街機市場競爭和高收入集中風險，鈊象 2000 年
跨入 PC 與線上遊戲產業，推出經典麻將線上遊戲「明星三缺一」、「唯舞獨尊」及「封神 Online」等，為公司的社交娛樂
場產品組合奠定堅實的基礎。2010 年起在智慧手機崛下時空背景下，鈊象 2015 年線上遊戲業務比重正式超越商用遊戲機，
而近五年隨自營 APP 產品玩家人數逐漸飽和，鈊象發展重心轉往海外授權，與當地代理商合作收取授權金以及服務費。
2025 年營收比重為：網路授權遊戲 68%、網路遊戲 APP 29%、商用遊戲機 2%。

### Page 12

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：軟體 2026 年 10 月 22 日 IGS 第12 頁，共 15頁

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分：鈊象整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於低風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排行在略微領先的位置，於
娛樂軟體業的公司中排名落後同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險：鈊象的整體曝險較低，略低於行業平均。人力資本、資料隱私與網路安全以及商業道德是值得關
注的重要 ESG 議題。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：鈊象在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力屬平均水準。該公司展現出非常強而
有力的環境政策和嚴格的 ESG 報告標準。其舉報人保護機制尚可完善，但仍有提升空間與保護機制的必要性。 ESG 治
理水準適中，設有 ESG 相關委員會，但其權限低於董事會。

持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。

材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。

3Q26 Review: “조삼모사” Again  
3Q26P 연결 실적은 매출액 6조 943억원(YoY -6.0%), 영업이익 4,520억원(YoY -46.2%, OPM 
7.4%)을 기록하며 컨센서스 대비 연결 매출액(7.5조원)과 영업이익(1.16조원) 모두 대폭 하회했다. 주
요 자회사가 양호한 실적을 기록했음에도 불구하고, 지상방산 실적이 매우 부진했다. 지상방산은 매출
액 1조 2,455억원(YoY -41.0%), 영업이익 217억원(YoY -96.2%, OPM 1.7%)을 기록했는데, 1)3Q 
생산 물량이 4Q 매출 인식되는 흐름 속, 고정비 부담이 컸었고, 2)낮은 수출 비중(3Q 48%, 2Q 58%)
과 국내외 사업의 부진한 믹스(국내 BEP 수준, 해외 OPM 11% 추정), 3)비우호적인 원/달러 환율, 4)
대전 사업장 사고 복구 및 기타 일회성 비용(300억원+160억원, 총 460억원) 등이 반영되었다.   
대전 사업장 정상화 시점과 사업 차질 영향은? 
대전 사업장은 내년 상반기 중 정상화될 예정이며, 보은과 여수 공장에서 생산 보완을 위한 조치가 진
행 중인 것으로 소통했다. 또한 대전 사업장은 국내 물량 위주이며, 수출 납기(천무, 천궁-II 추진체 
등)에는 지장이 없을 것이라고 언급했지만, 기타 사업장 램프업을 위한 인건비 증가 등, 수익성 악화는 
불가피할 것으로 예상된다.  
15%대의 매출 성장 가이던스는 달성이 가능할까? 
보수적으로 접근하는 것이 합리적이라는 판단이다. 당사는 ‘26E 지상방산 매출액을 일부 ‘27~28E로 
이연하면서 -6.1% 하향 조정했다. 사측은 폴란드 EC1 천무 인도 가이던스를 기존 40문에서 60문으
로 상향 조정하는 등 납품 속도가 예정대비 빨라진 것을 언급했다. 다만, 천무 EC1에서 미사일 물량 
비중이 높은 것으로 파악되기 때문에 생산 지연 가능성과 수출입 데이터 상 12월 출하 물량의 매출 인
식 이월 가능성을 반영했다. ‘27E는 폴란드 후속 사업과 루마니아 K9, 호주 레드백 등이 양산 페이즈
에 진입하며 성장세가 지속될 전망이다.    
투자의견 Buy, 목표주가 1,480,000원 유지 
실적 쇼크와는 별개로, 미국 K9MH 시제, 스페인 K9, 크로아티아 천무 등 수주는 서프라이즈라고 판
단된다. 분기말 수주잔고는 35조원(수출 비중 74%, 스페인 미포함)으로 중장기 성장성에 대한 이견은 
없다. 불가피한 분기 실적 변동으로 동사의 미래 가치를 예단하는 것은 시기상조라는 판단이다.

한화에어로스페이스 (012450)

## Document 257: [402340] SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대하이닉스 자사주 소각_31fbe439

Source PDF: all report pdf/[402340] SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대하이닉스 자사주 소각_31fbe439.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-09-30
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

SK 스퀘어 (402340/KS) 
하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대 
예정보다 빠른 SK 하이닉스 자사주 매입 
SK스퀘어의 자회사인 SK하이닉스는 지난 8월 20일부터 11월 19일까지 자사주 40
조원(2,407만주, 3.3%)을 매입 예정이다. 9월 29일까지 1,715만주, 예정 매입수량 
대비 71.3%를 매입 완료하였다. 매입 초기 매일 65만주 매입하다가 9월 10일부터는 
60만주를 매입하고 있는데, 이러한 추세가 유지된다면 10월 중순경 자사주 매입이 마
무리될 예정이다. 자사주 매입 후 소각 예정으로 소각이 완료되면 SK스퀘어의 SK하아
닉스에 대한 지분율은 20.0%에서 20.7%로 상승하게 된다.

자사주 매입 이후 추가적인 주주환원에 대한 기대 
SK하이닉스는 지난 8월 19일 정책기간(‘25년~’27년) 동안 발생하는 누적 FCF의 
50% 이상을 주주환원 하는 주주환원 정책을 발표한 바 있다. 26년 SK하이닉스의 예
상 FCF는 164조원(컨센서스 기준)으로 50%를 주주환원 할 경우 자사주 매입 금액
(40 조원)을 제외한 주주환원 재원은 42 조원이다. 잔여 주주환원 재원이 모두 배당으
로 지급된다면 SK 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후 SK 스퀘어의 지분율은 20.7%이므로 
SK스퀘어의 배당수입도 8.7조원에 달한다. 지난 3월 SK스퀘어는 ‘26년~’28년 경
상배당수입의 30% 이상을 주주에게 환원하는 정책을 발표했다. 보수적으로 30%만 
가정하더라도 SK스퀘어의 배당재원은 2.6조원(현 시가총액 대비 1.8%)이다. SK스퀘
어는 연초 주주총회에서 배당가능이익 확보 차원에서 자본준비금 5 조 8,900억원을 
이익잉여금으로 전입한 바 있다. 이는 26년 재무제표가 확정되는 27년 3월 이후 배당
가능이익으로 편입되므로 SK스퀘어의 주주환원 확대가 기대된다.

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 185 만원(유지) 
SK스퀘어의 순자산 가치 중 98% 이상이 SK하이닉스 지분가치이다. SK하이닉스는 
자사주 매입 이후 17.7% 주가가 상승했으나, SK 스퀘어 주가는 11.4% 상승했다. SK
하이닉스 자사주 매입·소각은 SK 스퀘어의 보유 지분가치 상승뿐 아니라 지분율도 상
승하기 때문에 자사주가 소각될 경우 SK스퀘어의 주가가 탄력적으로 움직일 가능성이 
높다. SK스퀘어 현재 NAV 대비 할인율은 43.7%, 12MF PBR 1.4배로 7월 이후 주
가 조정으로 밸류에이션 매력도 높아졌다. 투자의견 매수와 목표주가 185만원을 유지
한다.

2026-09-30

## Document 283: A股情绪见底了吗？

Source PDF: all report pdf/A股情绪见底了吗？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

中信证券认为，情绪指数于 10 ⽉ 30 ⽇跌破 10 进⼊恐慌区， A 股正式开启新⼀轮探底⾏情。
当前换⼿率仍在 1% 上⽅、融资买⼊降⾄⽜市以来最低、股债收益差创 2016 年以来最弱 ——
多项指标显⽰情绪尚未⻅底。后市或呈 “V 型底 ” 急跌抄底或 “U 型底 ” 漫⻓筑底两种演绎，救
市流动性⽀撑成关键变量。
摘要：
核⼼观点
情绪指数于 10 ⽉ 14 ⽇跌破 25 进⼊低迷区，标志着市场从震荡市转为熊市。 10 ⽉ 30 ⽇情绪指数再
次跌破 10 进⼊恐慌区，标志着 A 股将进⼊新⼀轮探底⾏情。市场陷⼊恐慌区之初可能由于被动去
杠杆或类似被动卖盘的担忧⽽出现恐慌性抛售和加速下跌的情况。要警惕市场陷⼊流动性危机
⽽出现负反馈问题，救市资⾦提供的流动性⽀持⾄关重要。当前情绪指数尚未⻅底，后续若情
绪指数⼤幅下滑并达到情绪冰点，则意味着市场到达 “V 型底 ” 底部，在最恐慌时刻将迎来最佳抄
底时机（如 24 年 1 ⽉）；若情绪指数在恐慌区窄幅震荡但未跌⾄情绪冰点，意味着市场仍将缓慢

向下探寻 “U 型底 ” 底部，需要耐⼼等待情绪指数回升⾄ 10 以上脱离恐慌区才能迎来做多机遇（如
24 年 6-9 ⽉）。
A 股情绪⻅底了吗？
换⼿率：在经历连续 3 个⽉缩量后，量能接近本轮⽜市以来最低⽔平。但当前换⼿率仍处于 1%
上⽅，意味着市场尚未充分缩量，后续存在⼆次探底⻛险。
偏股型基⾦新发量： 10 ⽉末偏股型基⾦新发量⼤幅上升⾄近年来偏⾼的⽔平，主要源⾃主动
ETF 基⾦集中发⾏的影响，剔除这⼀影响后，该指标仍处于低位。
融资买⼊占⽐：融资买⼊占⽐指标 10 ⽉初⼤幅下滑，⽉中已经降⾄本轮⽜市以来最低⽔平，下
旬窄幅震荡未能明显回升，显⽰杠杆资⾦情绪悲观。
隐含⻛险溢价：指标 10 ⽉延续上⾏趋势，从⽉初 3.08% 快速升⾄⽉末 3.51% ，显⽰权益资产性价
⽐显著提升。
股债收益差：指标 9 ⽉初快速下滑转负后基本都处于零轴下⽅， 10 ⽉末降⾄ -13.78% ，市场亏钱
效应为  2016 年  3 ⽉以来最强。
超 60MA ：指标 10 ⽉下滑后下旬已经跌破  20% 警戒线，反映市场资⾦⾯持续恶化，使得市场陷

⼊异常弱势状态（类似  201 8 年、 2024 年）。
超买超卖：8 ⽉以来⻓期处于零轴附近下⽅位置， 10 ⽉末加速下⾏跌⾄ -12.5% 附近，揭⽰当前市
场仍处于弱势震荡状态，短期可能出现技术性反弹，但⼤的超跌反弹动能仍然偏弱。
正⽂
2022 年 3 ⽉底，我们推出了中信建投策略 - 投资者情绪指数，由多个市场公开交易指标合成⽽
来。该指数在 A 股历史⾏情重要区间都较好地反映了当时市场情绪⽔平，并且极⾼和极低点能够
领先于⾏情反转，具有⼀定的预测能⼒。需要指出的是，该指数⽤于刻画市场的投资者情绪，
是同步指标，其预测性主要由投资者情绪对市场的预测性来体现。投资者情绪指数推出后，受
到了⼤量投资者的关注，因此我们从 2022 年 4 ⽉底开始，以⽉报的形式，对当前市场情绪进⾏跟
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

踪和展⽰，并给出分项核⼼情绪指标的历史⾛势和最新动向。 2024 年 8 ⽉，我们再度推出了专题
报告，回顾了情绪指数发布近两年半时间市场择时的实战效果，并总结了不同情绪状态下不同
市场⻛格的表现。
A 股情绪⻅底了吗？
10 ⽉投资者情绪指数并未根据季节效应规律在国庆节后出现回升，反⽽⼤幅下滑，这提⽰了 A 股
市场情绪异常快速下⾏和增量资⾦明显缺乏的问题。最终情绪指数于 10 ⽉ 14 ⽇跌破 25 进⼊低迷
区，结合万得全 A 指数⼤幅低于年线下⽅，标志着市场从震荡市转为熊市。 10 ⽉ 30 ⽇情绪指数再
次跌破 10 进⼊恐慌区，标志着 A 股将进⼊新⼀轮探底⾏情。
当情绪指数跌⼊恐慌区后如何应对？根据历史经验，市场陷⼊恐慌区之初可能由于被动去杠杆
或类似被动卖盘（险资、固收 + 等）的担忧⽽出现恐慌性抛售和加速下跌的情况。要警惕市场陷
⼊流动性危机⽽出现负反馈问题，救市资⾦提供的流动性⽀持⾄关重要。当前情绪指数尚未⻅
底，后续如果情绪指数⼤幅下滑并达到情绪冰点，则意味着市场到达 “V 型底 ” 的底部，在最恐慌

时刻将迎来最佳抄底时机（例如 2024 年 1 ⽉）；如果情绪指数在恐慌区窄幅震荡但未跌⾄情绪冰
点，意味着市场仍将缓慢向下探寻 “U 型底 ” 的底部，需要耐⼼等待情绪指数回升⾄ 10 以上脱离恐
慌区才能迎来做多机遇（例如 2024 年 6-9 ⽉）。
我们对各分项指标进⾏分析。七⼤指标中，换⼿率、偏股基⾦发⾏量、融资买⼊占⽐、隐含⻛
险溢价、股债收益差数据经过 5 ⽇平均平滑；超 60MA 、超买超卖两项指标原为周度数据，为了
更加灵敏现在统⼀进⾏⽇度化， 60 周均线实质上改成了 300 ⽇均线。下⽂均默认使⽤这⼀⼝径。

### Page 3

换⼿率：10 ⽉换⼿率低位震荡，但仍处于 1% 上⽅。在经历连续 3 个⽉缩量后，量能接近本轮⽜
市以来最低⽔平。但当前换⼿率仍处于 1% 上⽅，意味着市场尚未充分缩量，后续存在⼆次探底
⻛险。
偏股型基⾦新发量：剔除主动 ETF 基⾦集中发⾏影响后，偏股型基⾦新发量仍处于低位。 10 ⽉
末偏股型基⾦新发量⼤幅上升⾄近年来偏⾼的⽔平，主要源⾃主动 ETF 基⾦集中发⾏的影响，剔
除这⼀影响后，该指标仍处于低位。
融资买⼊占⽐：10 ⽉低位下滑，⽉中降⾄本轮⽜市以来最低⽔平。融资买⼊占⽐指标 10 ⽉初⼤
幅下滑，⽉中已经降⾄本轮⽜市以来最低⽔平，下旬窄幅震荡未能明显回升，显⽰杠杆资⾦情
绪悲观。

### Page 4

隐含⻛险溢价：10 ⽉⼤幅上⾏，权益资产性价⽐显著提升。指标 10 ⽉延续上⾏趋势，从⽉初
3.08% 快速升⾄⽉末 3.51% ，显⽰权益资产性价⽐显著提升。
股债收益差：10 ⽉⼤幅下⾏，⽉末降⾄ 2016 年 3 ⽉以来最低⽔平，市场亏钱效应显著。指标 9 ⽉
初快速下滑转负后基本都处于零轴下⽅， 10 ⽉末降⾄ -13.78% ，市场亏钱效应为 2016 年 3 ⽉以来
最强。
超 60MA ：10 ⽉低位下滑，下旬已经跌破 20% 警戒线，反映市场资⾦⾯持续恶化。该指标以中⻓
期视⻆刻画市场强弱状况，反映市场上收盘价在 60 周均线（ 300 ⽇均线）上⽅的个股⽐例。从历
史上看该指标超过 80%/ 低于 20% 往往意味着市场情绪的过热 / 过冷，⾏情有出现反转的可能。但
指标 10 ⽉下滑后下旬已经跌破 20% 警戒线，反映市场资⾦⾯持续恶化，使得市场陷⼊异常弱势
状态（类似 2018 年、 2024 年）。

中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

## Document 323: El Niño 2026–2027_ multiple pressures on prices and supplies (2026-09-30)

Source PDF: all report pdf/El Niño 2026–2027_ multiple pressures on prices and supplies (2026-09-30).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-09-30
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

30 September 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
EL NIÑO 2026–2027: MULTIPLE PRESSURES ON PRICES AND SUPPLIES
Hélène Drouot
The 2026–2027 El Niño climate event is expected to be one of the most intense in the last fifty years, with sea-surface-temperature
anomalies potentially exceeding +3°C in the equatorial Pacific. According to the World Meteorological Organisation (WMO), El Niño
is expected to persist until February 2027, with asymmetrical effects, as South-East Asia and northern Latin America will experience

droughts, while southern Brazil and Argentina could see heavy rainfall. Above all, El Niño is compounding the effects of global warming
and exacerbating existing extreme-weather events (droughts, floods and heatwaves). The economic effects of this climate shock mainly
play out through three avenues: agricultural commodity prices, energy and logistics (see heatmap).
Agricultural commodity prices: volatility is causing tensions, with inflationary effects

In India and South-East Asia, droughts will affect harvests, pushing up prices (particularly for coffee, cocoa, rice and palm oil). Conversely, in
southern Brazil and Argentina, heavy rainfall will boost soya and maize production, putting downward pressure on prices. According to the IMF,
El Niño causes global food prices to rise by between 5% (in a typical episode) and 8% (very high intensity) year-on-year, with the full impact being
felt 6 to 12 months after the climatic peak.

Therefore, for a given country, the weighting of food in its inflation index (CPI) plays a decisive role. As a result, all other things being equal, an 8%
rise in prices adds around 1 pp to inflation when food accounts for 13% of the CPI basket (average for countries shown in green on the heatmap),
1.8 pp when this share reaches 22% (yellow), 2.8 pp at 35% (orange) and nearly 4 pp above 49% (red).
The risks are skewed to the upside, as the 8% estimate does not take into account additional losses linked to droughts, floods and other extre -

me events, while policy responses to shocks (stockpiling by importers and increased export restrictions) may amplify and prolong price pres -
sures. The rice market between 2023 and 2024 illustrates this well, as the combination of rice-export restrictions imposed by India in July 2023
(in order to protect its domestic market) and of the drought in South-East Asia contributed to a 30% average increase in the price of rice over the
course of 2023 and kept global prices high for several months after the end of the El Niño episode.

Energy production sector: heavy reliance on hydroelectricity leaves the sector vulnerable to a second supply shock
Droughts will reduce hydroelectric power generation in Latin America and South-East Asia. As a result, some countries will have to turn to other,
more expensive solutions, such as fossil fuels, against a backdrop of already high oil and gas prices. This shift will increase global demand for
oil and put pressure on prices which, according to the IMF, could persist for up to four quarters after the initial El Niño shock. For example,

in Colombia, where hydropower accounts for 55% of electricity generation, a 30% drop in output (already seen in 2023–2024) could increase the
cost of electricity generation by 15 to 20% (assuming that the cost of thermal power generation is twice as high as hydropower). In Ecuador (where
hydropower accounts for 70% of electricity generation) and Vietnam (30%), electricity shortages are likely to disrupt local industries.
SOURCE: IMF, WORLD BANK, EMBER, BNP PARIBAS CALCULATIONS

Vulnerabilities differ, depending on a country’s dependence on food, energy and logistics

Reading note: Scores are standardised on a scale of 0 to 10 for ease of comparison between countries and supply channels.
- Food vulnerability: based on the weight of food in the CPI.
- Energy vulnerability: based on the share of hydropower in electricity generation.
- Logistics vulnerability: the ‘Trade and transport-related infrastructure’ component of the World Bank’s Logistics Perfor-

mance Index, which measures the quality of the infrastructure required for transport and international trade.

### Page 2

2
The bank for
a changing
world
Chart of the Week / 30 September 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
Logistics: transport infrastructure is another point of vulnerability
The final channel relates to disruptions to freight transport against a backdrop of already weakened value chains. The variable used in our
heatmap is a proxy for economies’ abilities to absorb a shock (particularly a climate-related one) affecting their transport infrastructure (for

example, by maintaining traffic flow, diverting or reorganising transport flows). The heatmap highlights the particularly high vulnerability
of Latin America. Its capacity to absorb a logistics shock is relatively limited, and there is significant exposure at a strategic chokepoint, the
Panama Canal, through which approximately 5% of global trade passes. Low rainfall in the canal basin has already led to the number of
daily transits being reduced in September 2026 (as in 2023–2024); further restrictions are expected in October (to 29 transits per day, down

from 36 in August), which will increase transport times and costs. Europe is also being affected, with low water levels on the Rhine and the
Danube already disrupting river transport and electricity generation.
Latin America and South-East Asia are hit hardest
Therefore, the economies most at risk are not necessarily those that will suffer the greatest climate shock, but those where multiple trans -
mission channels converge, including a high weighting of food prices in inflation, reliance on hydroelectricity, fragile transport infrastructure

and limited fiscal room for manoeuvre. Latin America and South-East Asia are home to some of the most vulnerable countries, particularly
due to their dependence on food prices and hydroelectricity. Colombia and Ecuador are noteworthy for their high exposure to energy risk,
while Ghana, India and the Philippines are some of the economies most vulnerable to a food shock. Latin America faces a combination of
vulnerabilities, such as high exposure to food and energy price shocks in some countries, which are compounded by logistics risks, as currently

illustrated by the restrictions on traffic through the Panama Canal.
helene.drouot@bnpparibas.com

## Document 348: Industrials_ AI Jitters Versus Booming Orders

Source PDF: all report pdf/Industrials_ AI Jitters Versus Booming Orders.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-10-28
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: Industrials: AI Jitters Versus Booming Orders
Date:    5 hours ago
Industrials: AI Jitters Versus Booming Orders

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Industrials: AI Jitters Versus
Booming Orders
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 28 Oct 2026
View in browser
Photo by Ant Rozetsky / Unsplash

I. AI Still Boosting Capital Goods Orders
The AI investment boom has been a double-edged sword for S&P 500
Industrials this year. Back in July, we noted that the sector was the
second-best performer among the S&P 500's 11 sectors, with a 17.7% ytd
gain, as investors favored companies supplying the equipment and
infrastructure needed for the AI buildout. Since then, however, intensifying

### Page 2

AI jitters have weighed on industrial stocks, fueled by a potentially toxic
trifecta of financial constraints: elevated borrowing costs, uncertain
investment returns, and concerns about whether sufficient capital will be
available to finance everyone's ambitious AI spending plans.
The corresponding reversal has been significant. As of October 27,
Industrials was up just 8.2% ytd, compared with a 12.8% gain for the S&P
500, slipping to sixth place among the 11 sectors (chart).

The sector's stock price index is trading below its 200-day moving average
but remains on a solid multi-year uptrend (chart).
Performance within the sector has been uneven so far this year, with rail
transportation leading, while industrial machinery and aerospace and
defense lagging (chart).

### Page 3

We think the recent selloff is overdone and continue to recommend
overweighting Industrials. For starters, the sector's earnings outlook
remains upbeat. Forward earnings rose 13.6% ytd to a record high last
week (chart).
The sector's forward profit margin is also at a record high of 11.7% (chart).

### Page 4

Analysts' net revisions of their revenues and earnings estimates have also
improved significantly in recent months, with both remaining historically
elevated (chart).
Furthermore, analysts still expect double-digit earnings growth of 17.6% in
2026 and 16.5% in 2027 (chart).

### Page 5

The sector's forward P/E has fallen sharply recently, but remains above
the overall market's valuation multiple (chart).
The AI investment boom continues despite the stock market's jitters about
the AI trade. The five major hyperscalers (Amazon, Alphabet, Microsoft,
Meta, and Oracle) are projected to spend nearly $800 billion on capex this
year and more than $1 trillion in 2027. So far this week, there has been
roughly $235 billion in announced AI financing, including up to $95 billion

from Meta, a $40 billion chip-and-server leasing arrangement involving
SpaceX, and a potential $100 billion IPO by Anthropic. We therefore
continue to recommend overweighting Industrials, which should continue
to benefit from the buildout.

### Page 6

Indeed, the AI spending boom is still boosting industrial activity, with much
more in the pipeline. Consider the following:
(1) Durable goods. September durable goods orders rose 1.8% m/m, the
largest increase in five months. Orders excluding transportation increased
0.5%, while core capital goods orders (nondefense excluding aircraft), a
key gauge of business investment, rose 0.5% and 13.5% y/y to another
record high (charts).
The major components of durable goods orders are at record highs

(chart).

### Page 7

Orders for machinery necessary to operate data centers are especially
strong (chart).
Unfilled orders for computers and electronic products rose to a record
$162.4 billion in June (chart).

### Page 8

(2) Regional business surveys. All five regional Fed manufacturing
surveys are now available for October. The regional manufacturing PMI
edged down to 13.8 from September but remained comfortably in
expansion territory, suggesting that the national ISM M-PMI likely
remained above 50.0 in October (chart).
The average prices-paid index across regional Fed surveys stood at 57.0,
near levels last seen in 2022. Meanwhile, the prices-received index
climbed to 31.6, suggesting that manufacturers are increasingly passing

higher input costs on to customers (chart).

### Page 9

II. US Economy
The industrial sector isn't the only driver of the US economy showing
remarkable strength. Another key driver of economic resilience is
consumer spending. Here is more:
(1) Consumer spending. Redbook same-store sales rose 9.7% y/y
during the week of October 16. That is well above the 2025 average of
5.8% (chart).
(2) Labor market. Consumers' perceptions of labor-market conditions
improved in October. The percentage saying jobs were plentiful rose to

26.7%, the highest since April (chart).

### Page 10

The latest weekly ADP data show private-sector employment rising by an
average of 16,750 jobs per week over the four weeks through October 10,
or roughly 67,000 jobs on a monthly basis (chart). The labor market
remains in good shape.
(3) GDP. Ahead of Thursday's release of the Q3-2026 US GDP report, the
Atlanta Fed's final GDPNow estimate suggests that the economy
expanded at a robust 3.4% annualized rate during the quarter, supported
by strong consumer spending and business investment (chart).

### Page 3

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics: November 2026: Key events, themes and trends to watch in the month ahead |  November 2026
US-China talks, a possible Trump-Xi-Putin trilateral meeting, and any signs of a China-Japan diplomatic
thaw will be critical watchpoints. The priorities of the summit between representatives of 21 Asia-Pacific
economies are set to include efforts to boost innovation, digital connectivity, supply-chain resilience and

support for an open multilateral trading system. During the summit, presidents Trump and Xi are expected to
meet for the third time this year. This meeting will provide signals on the state of China-US talks, including the
operationalisation of new bilateral trade and investment boards, and on the likelihood of another extension of
the Busan trade truce beyond January 2027. President Trump has also indicated he is open to a trilateral US-
China-Russia meeting during the summit, possibly covering nuclear arms control and the Russia-Ukraine war.

Investors will also closely watch whether Japanese Prime Minister Takaichi and President Xi meet on the
sidelines, following reports that Tokyo is hoping to arrange a meeting. However, the bar for a formal
breakthrough will likely remain high. As we noted in a report earlier this year, a full normalisation of relations
and an easing of Chinese economic restrictions – which include rare-earth export controls – may require a
lengthy period of diplomatic re-engagement and backchannel diplomacy.

A divided government could mean less change at home but more abroad. Third-party polling and betting
markets forecast Democrat control of the House, and an increasing probability of flipping the Senate. 80% of
respondents in our monthly poll now say living costs will influence their vote – the most since April. For
markets, a divided government could mean less domestic legislative change, but not necessarily a more
predictable US foreign policy. Losing Congress could leave theadministration’s geopolitical and geoeconomic

toolkit largely intact.
In our view, weapons stockpiles, inflationary pressures and long-end yields may matter more than Congress
in constraining theadministration’s choices in 2027 and 2028. A Democratic House could dial up oversight of
the administration, launch investigations, force votes on War Powers Resolutions, and use appropriations bills
to press for restrictions on departmental funding. This may generate a steady stream of attention-grabbing

headlines, but whether it decisively affects the event-driven risk that matters for markets (e.g. blocking a tariff
or preventing a limited military strike) remains in doubt. If domestic policymaking becomes more challenging,
foreign policy could become a top priority for the Trump administration.

### Page 4

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics: November 2026: Key events, themes and trends to watch in the month ahead |  November 2026
NewZealand’s general election is shaping up as a close and fragmented contest. Both the ruling National
and opposition Labour parties are polling at historically low levels, and smaller parties will likely determine
the next government. In a closely contested vote, investors could see coalition uncertainty and a wider

range of possible outcomes for tax, spending, infrastructure, energy and migration policy.
The National party is campaigning on a platform that would broadly preserve the current emphasis on
fiscal restraint, lower regulation and private-sector investment. A Labour-led government could place
greater weight on public services, housing and climate policy, alongside a proposed capital-gains tax on
investment property. However, because any government will rely on minor parties for survival, the final

policy mix will depend heavily on post-election negotiations.
The meeting will be a test of the EU’s ability to translate the competitiveness agenda into practical
reforms. The focus will be on Europe’s ability to restore competitiveness, mobilize investment and
maintain fiscal discipline. The Eurogroup brings together Euro-area finance ministers and is expected to
consider macroeconomic and fiscal developments, energy market pressures and progress towards the

Savings and Investments Union. ECOFIN brings together all EU finance ministers, and will focus on
financial integration, banking sector competitiveness, and economic coordination. To watch will be
tensions between fiscal consolidation and the need to fund defence, energy security and strategic
investment, alongside progress on integratingEurope’s fragmented capital markets and banking sectors. A
budget-focused Council meeting follows on 10 November.

Implementation of existing commitments, rather than new policy pledges, is expected to be the primary
focus of the next climate summit in Antalya. Building on the shift towards implementation at COP30,
COP31 will focus on translating existing climate commitments into practical action, including through
investment, financing and partnerships. For investors, the key watchpoint will be whether the summit can
deliver tangible commitments that translate existing plans into binding funding pledges for critical

infrastructure resilience, scaled-up renewable energy output, and climate finance.

### Page 5

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics: November 2026: Key events, themes and trends to watch in the month ahead |  November 2026
Melaka’s state election will offer a further gauge of Malaysia’s evolving electoral landscape after
strong recent state-level performances by Barisan Nasional (BN). The contest will also be watched for
its read-through to Prime Minister Anwar Ibrahim’s Pakatan Harapan (PH), following setbacks in other

state elections this year.
BN enters the election from a position of relative strength, while its electoral understanding with PAS
and its allies could provide an early indication of how political alignments are evolving ahead of the next
general election. However, Melaka’s local dynamics mean the result should not be treated as a direct
national forecast.
The anticipated signing of a landmark digital economy agreement is set to be one of the most

significant summit deliverables in years. The 49th ASEAN Summit in Manila will focus on economic
integration, energy security, supply-chain resilience, maritime security, the crisis in Myanmar, and
relations with external partners.
For markets, the formal signing of the ASEAN Digital Economy Framework Agreement (DEFA) is the key
event to watch. Designed as a legally binding treaty, DEFA aims to harmonize regional digital trade rules
and cross-border data flows. Successful implementation is expected to double the projected size of the

region’s digital economy by 2030, boosting it from a projected baseline of US$1 trillion to US$2 trillion
(up from approximately US$300 billion today).
The second EU-UK Summit will be an important test of whether the post-Brexit relationship can move
towards tangible outcomes. Prime Minister Andy Burnham will seek to finalize agreements on food and
agricultural standards, youth mobility, and linking emissions trading systems. Burnham has framed
relations as “not good enough.” To watch will be whether we see concrete deals versus declarations, and

whether leaders are prepared to make the concessions necessary to deepen ties.

### Page 6

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics: November 2026: Key events, themes and trends to watch in the month ahead |  November 2026
The upcoming local elections could serve as a political barometer ahead of the 2028 general election–
but only up to a point. Local elections in Taiwan are primarily driven by municipal ‘bread and butter’
economic issues; geopolitics and cross-Strait relations have historically not been at the forefront of

voters’ concerns. While this divergence limits theelections’ predictive value for the January 2028 general
vote, the results can provide a sense of the domestic balance of power.
Elections in Taiwan’s six special municipalities – Taipei, New Taipei, Taichung, Tainan, Taoyuan, and
Kaohsiung – will serve as particularly important watchpoints. Some recent polling suggests a potentially
close contest in Taipei between incumbent mayor Chiang Wan-an (Kuomintang) and legislator Shen Po-

yang (Democratic Progressive Party).
Spain’s snap general election could mark a major turning point for Spanish politics and the EU,
potentially ending eight years of Socialist-led government under Pedro Sánchez and bringing the
conservative People’s Party (PP) to power with support from the far-right Vox. Polling suggests PP is
ahead and a PP-Vox alignment is the only one projected to reach a majority. A rightward shift in one of
the EU’s largest economies could occur just as Europe enters a wider electoral cycle, potentially

complicating consensus on migration, climate policy, Ukraine and European integration.
The Forum comes at a pivotal moment in EU-Latin American relations. It is the EU’s first big commercial
push into Argentina since the EU-Mercosur trade deal began to be provisionally applied on 1 May. The
agreement opens new opportunities for trade, investment and market access. The EU is Argentina’s
largest source of foreign direct investment. Around 800 government, business and financial leaders will

focus on energy, critical minerals, agriculture and infrastructure. The EU’s Global Gateway investment
agenda provides a framework for mobilizing public and private capital. While a key outcome will be the
launch of a structured EU-Argentina investment dialogue, to watch will be the ability of Argentine
officials to allay concerns around policy stability and regulatory predictability.

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics: November 2026: Key events, themes and trends to watch in the month ahead |  November 2026
A potential Trump-Kim meeting in November could restart official bilateral dialogue, though North
Korea remains highly unlikely to abandon its nuclear program. South Korea’s National Intelligence
Service said in late September that it saw a roughly 50% chance of US-North Korea talks around the
APEC Summit in Shenzhen, although more recent reporting suggests the prospects may have

diminished. If talks do resume, they would only mark the first step in a long and highly uncertain
diplomatic process.
As we noted earlier this year, North Korea is highly unlikely to agree to denuclearization. Unless renewed
negotiations sidestep denuclearization in favor of a risk-reduction or coexistence framework, dialogue is
likely to stall, not least given Pyongyang’s constitutional pledge to continue expanding its nuclear
arsenal. This would make even a negotiated freeze on further expansion difficult to achieve.

## Document 427: United States _ A shock-resistant expansion (2026-09-24)

Source PDF: all report pdf/United States _ A shock-resistant expansion (2026-09-24).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-09-24
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

6
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoPerspectives / 3rd Quarter 2026 / September 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
A SHOCK-RESISTANT EXPANSION
US growth remains resilient despite successive shocks. According to our forecasts, it is expected to reach 2.2% in 2026 and
2.3% in 2027. Its main driver is still investment in artificial intelligence (AI), which has remained entirely unaffected by the
energy crisis. The labour market has seen a marked improvement in job creation and a fall in the unemployment rate. Core

inflation remains stubbornly high and above the Federal Reserve (Fed)’s target. Against this backdrop, the Fed has begun a
hiking cycle in September, which, according to our scenario, would bring the Fed Funds target to 4.50% (upper limit) by Q1
2027. At the same time, long-term rates are under upward pressure.
1  This includes: energy and communications; data centres (facilities); IT equipment and peripherals; communication equipment (equipment); software; and research and develop-

ment (intellectual-property products).
THE ENERGY SHOCK IS NOT HOLDING BACK GROWTH
Driven by AI-related investment, US growth is holding up despite ri -
sing energy inflation. We forecast GDP growth at 2.2% in 2026 and
2.3% in 2027 on an annual average basis. The AI-related investment
cycle1 (+14.8% y/y in H1 2026) is expected to continue, accompanied by
potential productivity gains. However, other private-investment seg -
ments (including residential property) remain sluggish. In addition, the

rising cost of capital could accentuate its ‘K-shaped’ profile. Household
consumption is expected, on the whole, to benefit from the rebound in
job creation and to remain above 2% y/y through to the end of 2027.
In H1 2026, it held up against resurging inflation due to an average
year-on-year increase of 11.5% in tax refunds and a savings rate stan-
ding at its lowest level since 2022 (2.8% in Q2).
RECORD CAPITAL-RAISING DRIVEN BY AI
This year, AI-related investments are driving massive capital-raising

by companies through loans, bond issues or share placements. This
trend is set to continue given the seemingly colossal scale of the fun -
ding requirements, even as risk premia in the bond market begin to
widen. According to their statements, the five tech giants could invest
nearly USD 900 bn this year and over USD 1,000 bn next year—more
than double the figure for 2025. Spurred on by regulatory easing, the
major US banks have announced that they will mobilise significant re-

sources to support projects considered strategic for the United States
(AI, defence, energy and infrastructure). While funding for the AI boom
appears unaffected by rising long-term interest rates, these latter are
likely to continue to weigh on household demand for mortgages. Since
the start of the conflict in the Middle East, 30-year mortgage rates
have risen by 78 bp (to 6.76% at the start of September). Conversely,
consumer lending remained buoyant in H1 2026 and is supporting

household spending.
TWO-SPEED GROWTH IN INDUSTRY
Industrial production rebounded slightly in 2025 (+1.1%), driven by AI
and aerospace. We forecast an acceleration to +1.5% in 2026 and +2%
in 2027. Investment in AI is bolstering the electronics sector, while the
(civil and military) aerospace sector is rebounding as order backlogs
are cleared and defence requirements rise. Conversely, sectors ex -
posed to rising tariffs (in particular, the steel, aluminium and auto -

motive sectors) are still on the back burner, contrary to the objective
of these measures. This positive momentum in industrial production,
while concentrated, is expected to continue, bolstered by the recove -
ry in business sentiment. The ISM Manufacturing Index has been in
expansionary territory since early 2026, despite ongoing pressures on
prices and supply chains.

### Page 2
